\begin{split}\Big\|\big\|\partial_{i}f_{n}(\tilde{Z}^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\big\|_{v,p_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}&\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\Big\|\sum_{j\leq p_{n}}\omega_{j}({\tilde{Z}^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}}_{1:n}})\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.\end{split}

This directly implies that
R_{n,1}^{*}=O(\frac{1}{n^{1/6}})
.
Moreover by using the chain rule we obtain that:

\begin{split}&\Big|\partial_{i,k_{1},k_{2}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\Big|\leq\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{n}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{n}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n}).\end{split}

Therefore as
\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})=1
we have for all
i\leq n

\begin{split}\Big\|\big\|\partial_{i}^{2}f_{n}(\tilde{Z}^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\big\|_{m,p_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{2\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{n}.\end{split}

this implies that
R_{n,2}^{*}=O(\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})/\sqrt{n})
.
Finally, by another application of the chain rule we have:

\begin{split}&\Big|\partial_{i,k_{1:3}}^{3}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\Big|\\
&\leq 2\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{3}}(x_{1:n})+[\mathbb{I}(k_{2}=k_{3})+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{3})\\
&+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})]\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3})\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n}).\end{split}

Therefore we obtain for all
i\leq n
that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|\max_{x\in[\bar{X}^{n},Z^{n}_{i}]\bigcup[0,\tilde{Y}^{n}_{i}]}\big\|\partial_{i}^{3}f_{n}(Z^{n,i,x}_{1:n})\big\|_{t,p_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{6\beta^{2}_{n}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\end{split}

This implies that
R_{3,n}^{*}=O(\frac{\beta_{n}^{3}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{n^{1/2}})

Finally by assumption we know that
\|\sup_{l\leq p_{n}}X^{n}_{1,l}\|_{L_{12}}<\infty
, hence the assumption
(H_{1}^{*})
holds as well as the desired result.

∎

Appendix H
Proofs from
Section

6

H.1
Proof of
4

Proof.

To get the desired results we use
Theorem

13
. Firstly we remark that the observations
X_{1}^{n}
take value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
. As there is a one to one mapping between
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
and
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}^{2}}
we could take
X_{1}^{n}
to take value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}^{2}}
. For ease of notations we do not do so, but note that the conditions of
Theorem

13
can alternatively be expressed for observations taking value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
.

We denote
g_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}X^{n}_{l,i,j}
;
and let
(\beta_{n})
be a sequence of positive reals satisfying (i)
\beta_{n}\rightarrow\infty
and (ii)
\beta_{n}=o(\log(p_{n})/n^{-1/4})
. We set
(f_{n})
to be the following sequence of functions:

f_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{1}{\beta_{n}log(p_{n})}\log\Big(\sum_{i\leq p_{n}}\big(\sum_{j\leq p_{n}}e^{\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}x_{l,i,j}}\big)^{-1}\Big).

We remark that they are three times differentiable and that following holds:

\begin{split}&\big\|g_{n}(Z^{n})-f_{n}(Z^{n})\|_{L_{1}}\leq\frac{2}{\beta_{n}}\\
&\big\|g_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})-f_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})\|_{L_{1}}\leq\frac{2}{\beta_{n}}.\end{split}

Therefore hypothesis
(H_{0})
holds and we only need to check that hypothesis
(H_{1}^{*})
also holds to be able to use
Theorem

13
. For ease of notations we write
\hat{R}_{i,j}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X_{l,i,j}
and denote

\omega_{i,j}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{e^{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\hat{R}_{i,j}}}{\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}e^{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\hat{R}_{i,k}}};\qquad\overline{\omega}_{i}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{\big[\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}e^{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\hat{R}_{i,k}}\big]^{-1}}{\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}\big[\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}e^{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\hat{R}_{i,k}}\big]^{-1}}.

We remark that the following holds:

\sum_{k,j\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega}_{k}(x_{1:n})=1,\quad\sum_{j\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k,j}(x_{1:n})=1

(262)

By the chain rule, we remark that for all
k,j\leq p_{n}
we have:

\partial_{i,k,j}f_{n}(x_{1:n})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\omega_{k,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega}_{k}(x_{1:n}).

Therefore as
\sum_{k,j\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega}_{k}(x_{1:n})=1
, this implies that:

\max_{l\leq n}\Big\|\|\partial_{l,i,j}f_{n}(Z^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\|_{v,p_{n}^{2}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

This implies that
R_{n,1}^{*}=O(\frac{1}{n^{1/6}}).

Moreover by another application of the chain rule we have for all
i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq p_{n}
and all
l\leq n
:

\begin{split}&\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\\
&=\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\mathbb{I}(i_{1}=i_{2})}{\sqrt{n}}\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1}}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\Big[\mathbb{I}(j_{1}=j_{2})-2\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\Big]\\
&-\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1}}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\frac{\log(p_{n})\beta_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega}_{i_{2}}(x_{1:n}).\end{split}

Therefore we have by combining this with
eq.

262
and the fact that
\sum_{k,j\leq p_{n}}|\partial_{l,k,j}f_{n}(x_{1:n})|\leq 1/\sqrt{n}
we have

\max_{l\leq n}\Big\|\big\|\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}}^{2}f_{n}(Z^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\big\|_{m,p_{n}}\Big\|\leq\frac{4\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{n}.

Therefore we have
R_{2,n}^{*}=O(\frac{\log(p_{n})\beta_{n}}{\sqrt{n}})
. Finally, by a last application of the chain rule for all
i_{1:3},j_{1:3}\leq p_{n}
we have:

\begin{split}&\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2},i_{3},j_{3}}^{3}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\\
&=\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})\mathbb{I}(i_{1}=i_{2})}{\sqrt{n}}\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{3},j_{3}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\Big[\mathbb{I}(j_{1}=j_{2})-2\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\Big]\\
&-\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{3},j_{3}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\frac{\log(p_{n})\beta_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega_{i_{2}}}(x_{1:n})\\
&-2\frac{\log(p_{n})^{2}\beta_{n}^{2}\mathbb{I}(i_{1}=i_{2}=i_{3})}{n}\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1}}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\Big[\mathbb{I}(j_{2}=j_{3})-\omega_{i_{3},j_{3}}(x_{1:n})\Big]\\
&-\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1}}\mathbb{I}(i_{2}=i_{3})f_{n}(x_{1:n})\frac{\log(p_{n})^{2}\beta^{2}}{n}\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega_{i_{2}}}(x_{1:n})\times\Big[\mathbb{I}(j_{2}=j_{3})-2\omega_{i_{3},j_{3}}(x_{1:n})\Big]\\
&+\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1}}f_{n}(x_{1:n}){\omega_{i_{2},j_{2}}}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega_{i_{2}}}(x_{1:n}){\omega_{i_{3},j_{3}}}(x_{1:n})\overline{\omega_{i_{3}}}(x_{1:n}).\end{split}

Therefore by combining this with
eq.

262
and the fact that
\sum_{i_{1:2}\leq p_{n},j_{1:2}\leq p_{n}}\left|\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}}^{2}f_{n}(Z^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\right|\leq\frac{4\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})}{n}
we have

\begin{split}&\max_{l\leq n}\left\|\max_{z\in[\bar{X}^{n},\tilde{Z}_{1}^{n}]\bigcup[\bar{X}^{n},Y_{1}^{n}]}\left\|\partial_{l,i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2},i_{3},j_{3}}^{3}f_{n}(Z^{i,z})\right\|_{t,p_{n}}\right\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{10\log(p_{n})^{2}\beta_{n}^{2}}{n^{3/2}}.\end{split}

This implies that
R_{3,n}^{*}=O(\frac{\log(p_{n})^{2}\beta_{n}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}})
.
Therefore if we choose
\beta_{n}=\frac{n^{1/6}}{{\log(p_{n})}^{2/3}}
then
by using
Theorem

13
guarantees that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}(g_{n}(Z^{n}),g_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})|X^{n})\Big\|_{L_{1}}=O\left(\frac{{\log(p_{n})}^{2/3}}{n^{1/6}}\right)\end{split}

Moreover by definition of
\tilde{Y}^{n}
we have
\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{1,i,j}^{n}\mid X^{n}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{Y}_{1,i,j}^{n}\mid X^{n}\right)
therefore
\theta_{Z^{n}}=\theta_{\tilde{Y}^{n}}
. By combining this with
lemma

11
we conclude that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}(g_{n}(Z^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{Z^{n}},~g_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{\tilde{Y}^{n}}|X^{n})\Big\|_{L_{1}}=O\left(\frac{{\log(p_{n})}^{2/3}}{n^{1/6}}\right)\end{split}

Moreover by following the exact same line of reasoning we can prove that:

\begin{split}&\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(g_{n}(\tilde{Z}^{n}),g_{n}(Y^{n})\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}=O\left(\frac{{\log(p_{n})}^{2/3}}{n^{1/6}}\right)\\
&\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(g_{n}(\tilde{Z}^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{\tilde{Z}^{n}},~g_{n}(Y^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{0}\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|=O\left(\frac{{\log(p_{n})}^{2/3}}{n^{1/6}}\right)\end{split}

Therefore according to
1
if
(t_{n,\alpha})
is a sequence chosen such that:

P\left(\left|g_{n}(\tilde{Z}^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{\tilde{Z}^{n}}\right|\geq t_{n,\alpha}\mid X^{n}\right)=\alpha

then for all sequence
(\epsilon_{n})
satisfying
\epsilon_{n}\downarrow 0
and
\left(\frac{\log(p_{n})^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{1/3}=o(\epsilon_{n})
we have

\limsup_{n\rightarrow 0}P\left(\left|g_{n}(X_{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{0}\right|\geq t_{n,\alpha}+\epsilon_{n}\mid X^{n}\right)\leq\alpha.

∎

Lemma 25
.

Let
(p_{n})
be a sequence of integers satisfying
\log(p_{n})=o(n^{1/4})
. Define
(X^{n}_{i})
to be a triangular array of sequences of i.i.d random variables taking value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
. Suppose that
\|\sup_{l_{1},l_{2}\leq p_{n}}|X_{1,l_{1},l_{2}}^{n}|\|_{L_{12}}<\infty
and that
\frac{\log(p_{n})}{\sqrt{n}}=o\Big[\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|\Big]
. We denote:
\hat{\theta}_{n}(x_{1:n})=\frac{1}{n}\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X_{l,i,j}^{n}
.
Then the following holds

\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(Z^{n})-\theta_{Z^{n}}\Big],~\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\theta_{Y^{n}}\Big]\mid X^{n}\right)\Big\|_{L_{1}}=o(1);

where for a process
(Y^{\prime}_{1:n})
we have written, by abuse of notations,

\theta_{Y^{\prime}}:=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}~\mathbb{E}\big[Y^{{}^{\prime}}_{l,i,j}|X^{n}\big].

Proof.

Firstly, we remark that
4
imply that:

\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(Z^{n})-\theta_{Z^{n}}\Big],~\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})-\theta_{\tilde{Y}^{n}}\Big]\mid X^{n}\right)\Big\|_{L_{1}}=o(1).

Therefore to get the desired result we only need to prove that:

\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\theta_{Y^{n}}\Big],~\sqrt{n}\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})-\theta_{\tilde{Y}^{n}}\Big]\mid X^{n}\right)\Big\|_{L_{1}}=o(1).

We denote
I,J\leq p_{n}
the unique pair of indices satisfying
\mathbb{E}\left(X^{n}_{1,I,J}\right)=\theta_{0}
; and define:

\epsilon_{n}:=\frac{\log(p_{n})\left\|\max_{i,j}\left|X^{n}_{1,i,j}\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}}{\sqrt{n}\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}.

Using
Lemma

8
we know that there is a constant
C
such that

\begin{split}&P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Y^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(Y^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|\geq\frac{\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}{4}\right)\\
&=P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Y^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(Y^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|\geq\frac{\log(p_{n})\left\|\max_{i,j}\left|X^{n}_{1,i,j}\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}}{4\epsilon_{n}\sqrt{n}}\right)\\
&\overset{}{\leq}{C\epsilon_{n}^{2}}\rightarrow 0.\end{split}

We remark that if
\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})
is distinct from
\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}Y^{n}_{l,I,J}
this means that there are indexes
i,j\leq p_{n}
such that
\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Y^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(Y^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|
is bigger than
\frac{\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}{2}
. Therefore we have:

\begin{split}&P\left(\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})\neq\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}Y^{n}_{l,I,J}\right)\\
&\leq P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Y^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(Y^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|\geq\frac{\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}{2}\right)\\
&\leq{C\epsilon_{n}^{2}}\to 0.\end{split}

Similarly we have

\begin{split}&P\left(\hat{\theta}_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})=\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}\tilde{Y}^{n}_{l,I,J}\right)\\
&\leq P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(X^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|\geq\frac{\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}{4}\right)\\
&+P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Y^{n}_{l,i,j}-\mathbb{E}(Y^{n}_{l,i,j})\right|\geq\frac{\inf_{(i,j)\neq(l,k)}\big|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,l,k}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[X^{n}_{1,i,j}\big]\big|}{4}\right)\\
&\leq{2C\epsilon_{n}^{2}}\to 0.\end{split}

Therefore with high-probability we have:

\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})=\hat{\theta}_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})-\theta_{\tilde{Y}^{n}}+\theta_{Y^{n}}.

Using the dominated convergence theorem we thus obtain that

\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big[~\sqrt{n}\big[{\hat{\theta}_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{n})}-\theta_{\tilde{Z}^{n}}\big],\sqrt{n}\big[{g_{n}(Y^{n})}-\theta_{Y^{n}}\big]\mid X^{n}\Big]\Big\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.

∎

Lemma 26
.

Let
(p_{n})
be a sequence of integers satisfying
\log(p_{n})=o(n^{1/7})
. Define
(X^{n}_{i})
to be a triangular array of i.i.d random variables taking value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
. Suppose that
\sup_{n}\|\sup_{l_{1},l_{2}\leq p_{n}}|X_{1,l_{1},l_{2}}^{n}|\|_{L_{12}}<\infty
. We denote
t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}
a constant such that:

P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\big|N^{n}_{i,j}|\geq t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}\right)\leq\beta/4,

where
(N^{n})
is a sequence of gaussian matrices satisfying
\rm{Cov}(N^{n}_{i,j},N^{n}_{k,l})=\rm{Cov}(X^{n}_{i,j},X^{n}_{k,l})
and write
\hat{\theta}_{n}(x_{1:n})=\frac{1}{n}\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X_{l,i,j}^{n}
. Choose
t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})
such that the following holds:

P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Z^{n})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}(Z^{n})\mid X^{n})\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})\mid X^{n}\right)\leq\beta/4.

The following holds

\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(X^{n})-\theta_{X^{n}}\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})+3t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\right)\leq\beta;

where for a process
(Y^{\prime}_{1:n})
we have written, by abuse of notations,

\theta_{Y^{\prime}}:=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}~\mathbb{E}\big[Y^{{}^{\prime}}_{l,i,j}|X^{n}\big].

Proof.

We want to use
Theorem

5
to prove this result. By the triangle inequality we know that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\theta_{Y^{n}}\right|\leq\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}({Y^{n}}))\right|+\sqrt{n}\left|\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}({Y^{n}}))-\theta_{Y^{n}}\right|.

(263)

This implies that:

\displaystyle P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\theta_{Y^{n}}\right|\leq t_{\beta/2}(X^{n})+3t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\right)

(264)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}({Y^{n}}))\right|\leq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})+2t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta/2\right)

(265)

\displaystyle+P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}({Y^{n}}))-\theta_{Y^{n}}\right|\leq t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta/2\right).

(266)

We bound the first term using
Theorem

5
and use arguments that are inspired by the proof of
Theorem

5
to bound the second.

Firstly, we remark that in
4
we proved that
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}
satisfies
1
. Moreover we note that for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}\times p_{n}}
we have

\sqrt{n}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n}+\frac{x}{\sqrt{n}})-\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})\right)\right|\leq 2\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}|x_{i,j}|.

Therefore according to
Theorem

5
we know that:

\displaystyle\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n}))\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(Y^{n})+2t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\right)\leq\beta/2.

(267)

Moreover we note that:

\sqrt{n}\left|\mathbb{E}(\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n}))-\theta_{X^{n}}\right|\leq\sup_{i,j}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X_{l,i,j}^{n}-\mathbb{E}(X_{1,i,j}^{n})\right|.

Using
[
16
]
we know that there is a constant
C
that does not depend on
n
such that the following holds:

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\Big|P\left[\sup_{i,j}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}X_{l,i,j}^{n}-\mathbb{E}(X_{1,i,j}^{n})\right|\geq t\right]-P(\max_{i,j}|N^{n}_{i,j}|\geq t)\Big|

(268)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\log(p_{n})^{7/6}\max(\|\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}|X^{n}_{1,i,j}|\|_{L_{4}}^{4},1)}{n^{1/6}}\to 0.

(269)

Therefore by combining
eq.

270
and
eq.

268
we obtain that:

\displaystyle\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\sqrt{n}\left|\hat{\theta}_{n}(Y^{n})-\theta_{X^{n}}\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(Y^{n})+3t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\right)\leq\beta.

(270)

∎

H.2
Proof of
5

We separately prove each statement. Firstly we examine the case where both
(H_{0}^{\rm{minmax}})
and
(H_{1}^{\rm{minmax}})
are assumed to hold.

Proof.

The first step of the proof is to approximate
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\cdot)
by a discrete version and use
Lemma

25
to prove the desired results. In this goal, choose
(\epsilon_{n})
and
(\delta_{n})
to be two sequences satisfying:

\epsilon_{n}=\frac{1}{n^{1/8}\sqrt{\log(nC_{n})d^{\prime}_{n}C_{n}c_{n}}};\qquad\delta_{n}=\frac{1}{(C_{n}n)^{3/2}}.

We denote
\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}:=\{\omega_{i}^{*},~i\leq p_{n}\}
a maximal
\epsilon_{n}
-packing of
B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
, it is well known that
p_{n}\leq\Big(\frac{2}{\epsilon_{n}}+1\Big)^{d^{\prime}_{n}}
. Moreover we remark that for all
\theta\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
there is an
\omega^{*}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}
such that
\left\|\omega^{*}-\omega\right\|\leq\epsilon_{n}
. Indeed if this would not be the case then
\{\omega\}\bigcup\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}
would also be a packing set of
B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
contradicting the fact that we assumed it to be maximal. Similarly we can find a
\delta_{n}
-covering net of
B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
:
\Omega^{\delta_{n}}:=\{\omega^{\prime}_{i},~i\leq q_{n}\}
with
q_{n}\leq\left(\frac{2}{\delta_{n}}+1\right)^{d^{\prime}_{n}}
.

By exploiting conditions
(H_{0}^{\rm{minmax}})
for all
\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
we have

\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{n}}}\inf_{\omega_{1}^{*},\omega_{2}^{*}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\|f_{n}(x,\omega_{1},\omega_{2})-f_{n}(x,\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\big\|_{L_{\infty}}<C_{n}\epsilon_{n};

(271)

and by using
(H_{1}^{\rm{minmax}})
we remark that

\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i,j)\neq(k,l)\end{subarray}}\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[f_{n}(Y_{1}^{n},\omega^{*}_{k},\omega^{*}_{l})\Big]-\mathbb{E}\Big[f_{n}(Y_{1}^{n},\omega^{*}_{i},\omega^{*}_{j})\Big]\Big|>c_{n}\epsilon_{n}.

(272)

We define
g_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}f_{n}(x_{l},\omega^{*}_{i},\omega^{*}_{j})
and remark that it approximates
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\cdot).
Indeed by combining
eq.

271
and
Lemma

8
we know that there is a constant
K
such that :

\displaystyle\left\|g_{n}(\xi^{n})-\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\xi^{n})\right\|_{L_{1}}

(273)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sup_{\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}}\sup_{\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega_{1},\omega_{2})-f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}

(274)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}\delta_{n}\sqrt{n}+\left\|\sup_{\omega^{\prime}_{1},\omega^{\prime}_{2}\in\Omega^{\delta_{n}}}\sup_{\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{\prime}_{1},\omega^{\prime}_{2})-f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}

(275)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}^{-1/6}n^{-1/6}+K\log(q_{n})C_{n}\epsilon_{n}

(276)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}^{-1/6}n^{-1/6}+K\log(\frac{2}{(C_{n}n)^{3/2}}+1)d^{\prime}_{n}C_{n}\epsilon_{n}\rightarrow 0.

(277)

Therefore if we establish that the bootstrap method is consistent for
(g_{n})
we will have demonstrated that it is for
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(X_{1:n}^{n})
.
For ease of notations we denote

\theta^{dis}_{Z^{n}}:=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\mathbb{E}\big[f_{n}(Z^{n}_{1},\omega^{*}_{i},\omega^{*}_{j})|\xi^{n}\big],\qquad\theta^{dis}_{\xi^{n}}:=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\mathbb{E}\big[f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{1},\omega^{*}_{i},\omega^{*}_{j})\big].

We remark that as
\log(p_{n})=o(n^{1/4})

and as
eq.

272
guarantees that

\log(p_{n})/\sqrt{n}=o(\min_{(i,j)\neq(k,l)}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(f_{n}(\xi_{1}^{n},\omega_{i},\omega_{j})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(f_{n}(\xi_{1}^{n},\omega_{l},\omega_{k})\right)\right|)

then
Lemma

25
guarantees that

\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big[g_{n}(\xi^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta^{dis}_{\xi^{n}},~{g_{n}(Z^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta^{dis}_{Z^{n}}}\Big|\xi^{n}\Big]\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.

Hence by a last application of
eq.

273
we get the desired result.
∎

We now examine the case where we only assume that
(H_{0}^{\rm{minmax}})
holds.

Proof.

The first step is to approximate
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\cdot)
by a discrete version and use
Lemma

26
to prove the desired results. In this goal, choose
(\epsilon_{n})
and
(\delta_{n})
to be two sequences satisfying:

\epsilon_{n}=\frac{1}{n^{1/8}\sqrt{\log(nC_{n})d^{\prime}_{n}C_{n}}};\qquad\delta_{n}=\frac{1}{(C_{n}n)^{3/2}}.

Similarly as previously, we denote
\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}:=\{\omega_{i}^{*},~i\leq p_{n}\}
a maximal
\epsilon_{n}
-packing of
B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
with
p_{n}\leq\Big(\frac{2}{\epsilon_{n}}+1\Big)^{d^{\prime}_{n}}
. Similarly we can find a
\delta_{n}
-covering net of
B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
:
\Omega^{\delta_{n}}:=\{\omega^{\prime}_{i},~i\leq q_{n}\}
with
q_{n}\leq\left(\frac{2}{\delta_{n}}+1\right)^{d^{\prime}_{n}}
.

As above, we remark that for all
\theta\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
there is an
\omega^{*}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}
such that
\left\|\omega^{*}-\omega\right\|\leq\epsilon_{n}
.
By exploiting conditions
(H_{0}^{\rm{minmax}})
we obtain for all
\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}
that:

\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{n}}}\inf_{\omega_{1}^{*},\omega_{2}^{*}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\|f_{n}(x,\omega_{1},\omega_{2})-f_{n}(x,\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\big\|_{L_{\infty}}<C_{n}\epsilon_{n}.

(278)

We define
g_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\min_{i\leq p_{n}}\max_{j\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}f_{n}(x_{l},\omega^{*}_{i},\omega^{*}_{j})
and remark that it approximates
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\cdot).
Indeed by combining
eq.

278
and
Lemma

8
we know that there is a constant
K
such that :

\displaystyle\left\|g_{n}(\xi^{n})-\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(\xi^{n})\right\|_{L_{1}}

(279)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sup_{\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in B_{d^{\prime}_{n}}^{1}}\sup_{\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega_{1},\omega_{2})-f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}

(280)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}\delta_{n}\sqrt{n}+\left\|\sup_{\omega^{\prime}_{1},\omega^{\prime}_{2}\in\Omega^{\delta_{n}}}\sup_{\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2}\in\Omega^{\mathcal{P}}_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{\prime}_{1},\omega^{\prime}_{2})-f_{n}(\xi^{n}_{i},\omega^{*}_{1},\omega^{*}_{2})\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}

(281)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}^{-1/6}n^{-1/6}+K\log(q_{n})C_{n}\epsilon_{n}

(282)

\displaystyle\leq C_{n}^{-1/6}n^{-1/6}+K\log(\frac{2}{(C_{n}n)^{3/2}}+1)d^{\prime}_{n}C_{n}\epsilon_{n}\rightarrow 0.

(283)

Therefore if we establish the desired result for
(g_{n})
it will also hold for
\sqrt{n}\hat{\theta}_{n}(X_{1:n}^{n})
.
We write
X_{1}^{n}:=(f_{n}(\xi_{1}^{n},\omega_{i},\omega_{i}))_{i,j\leq p_{n}}
and denote
t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}
a constant such that:

P\left(\max_{i,j\leq p_{n}}\big|N^{n}_{i,j}|\geq t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}\right)\leq\beta/4,

where
N^{n}
is a gaussian matrix with
\rm{Cov}\left(N_{i,j}^{n},N_{k,l}^{n}\right)=\rm{Cov}\left(X_{i,j}^{n},X_{k,l}^{n}\right)
. Choose
t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})
such that the following holds:

P\left(\left|g_{n}(Z^{n})-\mathbb{E}(g_{n}(Z^{n})\mid X^{n})\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})\mid X^{n}\right)\leq\beta/4.

We remark that
\log(p_{n})=o(n^{1/7})

and that
g_{n}
satisfies all of the conditions of
Lemma

26
. Therefore according to
Lemma

26
we know that:

\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\left|g_{n}(Z^{n})-\mathbb{E}(g_{n}(Z^{n})\mid X^{n})\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})+3t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\mid X^{n}\right)\leq\beta.

We note that as
\|g_{n}(X^{n})-\sqrt{n}\theta_{n}(X^{n})\|_{L_{1}}
we therefore have that

\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\left|g_{n}(Y^{n})-\mathbb{E}(g_{n}(Y^{n}))\right|\geq t_{\beta/4}(X^{n})+3t_{g,n}^{\beta/4}+\delta\mid X^{n}\right)\leq\beta.

Finally we note that
t^{*,\beta/4}_{g,n}\geq t^{\beta/4}_{g,n}
which implies the desired result.

∎

Appendix I
Proofs from
Section

7

I.1
Proof of
6

Proof.

For simplicity we write
\Theta_{n}:=\{\theta_{k},~k\leq p_{n}\}
and for all
\theta\in\Theta_{n}
we denote

g_{\theta}(x_{1:2},y_{1:2}):=K_{\theta}(x_{1},x_{2})+K_{\theta}(y_{1},y_{2})-K_{\theta}(x_{1},y_{2})-K_{\theta}(y_{1},x_{2});

D_{n}:=4\max\left(\big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}K_{\theta_{k}}(X_{1,1}^{M},X_{1,1}^{M})\big\|_{L_{120}},\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k},~1\right).

Using Mercer’s theorem we note that there are orthonormal eigenfunctions
(\psi_{l,k})
and positive eigenvalues
(\lambda_{l,k})
such that:

K_{\theta_{k}}(\cdot,\cdot):=\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}\psi_{l,k}(\cdot)\psi_{l,k}(\cdot).

As we assumed that
\max_{k}\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}<\infty
then there is a sequence
(l_{n})
such that:

\max_{k}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}=o\left(\min(\lambda_{n},1)^{2}(\beta_{n}np_{n}D_{n}^{3})^{-1}\right).

We show that we can suppose without loss of generality that the kernels
(K_{\theta_{k}})
are of rank
l_{n}
or less. In this goal, we denote
K_{\theta_{k}}^{*}(\cdot,\cdot)
the following kernels
K_{\theta_{k}}^{*}(\cdot,\cdot):=\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\psi_{l,k}(\cdot)\psi_{l,k}(\cdot)
and shorthand

H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M}):=K^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{j,1},X^{M}_{i,1})+K^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{j,2},X^{M}_{i,2})-K^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{j,1},X^{M}_{i,2})-K^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{j,2},X^{M}_{i,1});

p^{*}_{\theta}(X^{M}):=\frac{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta}(X^{M})}{\frac{4}{|n|^{3}}\sum_{i\leq n}\big[\sum_{j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*,\theta}(X^{M})]^{2}-\frac{4}{n^{4}}\Big[\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*,\theta}(X^{M})\Big]^{2}+\lambda_{n}}.

\omega^{*}_{k}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{e^{\beta_{n}p^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M})}}{\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}e^{\beta_{n}p^{*}_{\theta_{k}^{\prime}}(X^{M})}}

We prove that:

\left\|nT_{n}(X^{M})-\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(X^{M})\right\|_{L_{1}}\to 0

and that

\left\|nT_{n}(Z^{M})-\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(Z^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(Z^{M})\right\|_{L_{1}}\to 0.

In this goal, we remark that:

\begin{split}\sup_{i,j\leq n}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)\right|\right\|_{L_{4}}&\leq\sup_{i,j\leq n}\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\left\|{\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)}\right\|_{L_{4}}\\
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}4\sup_{i,j\leq n}\left\|{\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|K^{*}_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{i,1},X^{M}_{j,2})-K_{\theta_{k}}(X^{M}_{i,1},X^{M}_{j,2})\right|}\right\|_{L_{4}}\\
&\leq 4\sup_{i,j\leq n}\left\|{\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\left|\psi_{l,k}(X_{i,1}^{M})\psi(X_{j,2}^{M})\right|}\right\|_{L_{4}}\\
&\overset{}{\leq}4p_{n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\sup_{i\leq n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n},l\in\mathbb{N}}\left\|{{\psi_{l,k}(X_{i,1}^{M})^{2}}}\right\|_{L_{4}}\\
&\overset{}{\leq}4p_{n}D_{n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\end{split}

where to get (a) we used the fact that
X_{i,1}^{M}
has the same distribution than
X_{i,2}^{M}
.

We remark that the function
\sigma_{n}:(x_{1},\dots,x_{p_{n}})\rightarrow\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\frac{e^{\beta_{n}x_{k}}}{\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}e^{\beta_{n}x_{k^{\prime}}}}
is Lipchitz in the max norm:
\left|\sigma_{n}(x)-\sigma_{n}(y)\right|\leq\beta_{n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\right|\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|x_{k}-y_{k}\right|.
Therefore coupling this with the triangle inequality we get that

\begin{split}&~\left\|nT_{n}(X^{M})-\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(X^{M})\right\|_{L_{1}}\\
\leq&\left\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\leq p_{n}\\
i,j\leq n\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)\omega_{k}(X^{M})\right\|_{L_{1}}+\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\leq p_{n}\\
i,j\leq n\end{subarray}}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\left(\omega_{k}(X^{M})-\omega^{*}_{k}(X^{M})\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\\
\overset{(a)}{\leq}&n\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j\leq n}\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}+2n\beta_{n}\sup_{i,j\leq n}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|p_{\theta_{k}}^{*}(X^{n})-p_{\theta_{k}}(X^{n})\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}.\end{split}

The first term of
section

I.1
is bounded by:

n\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j\leq n}\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)\right|\right\|_{L_{1}}\leq np_{n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\rightarrow 0.

Moreover we can bound the second term of
section

I.1
using the triangular inequality,
section

I.1
and the fact that

\left(\frac{4}{|n|^{3}}\sum_{i\leq n}\big[\sum_{j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*,\theta}]^{2}-\frac{4}{n^{4}}\Big[\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*,\theta}\Big]^{2}+\lambda_{n}\right)^{-1}\geq\lambda_{n},\qquad\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\right|\right\|_{L_{4}}\leq D_{n}.

Indeed we have,

\begin{split}&~2n\beta_{n}\sup_{i,j\leq n}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|p_{\theta_{k}}^{*}(X^{n})-p_{\theta_{k}}(X^{n})\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}\\
\overset{}{\leq}&\frac{2n}{\lambda_{n}}\beta_{n}D_{n}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j\leq n}\left(H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})-H^{\theta_{k}}_{i,j}\right)\right|\right\|_{L_{2}}+\frac{2n\beta_{n}}{\lambda_{n}^{2}}D_{n}^{2}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\Big\|\frac{4}{|n|^{3}}\sum_{i\leq n}\Big(\big[\sum_{j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})]^{2}\\
&-\big[\sum_{j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{\theta_{k}}]^{2}\Big)-\frac{4}{n^{4}}\left(\Big[\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\Big]^{2}-\Big[\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{\theta_{k}}\Big]^{2}\right)\Big\|_{L_{2}}\\
\leq&4np_{n}\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\geq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\big[1+\frac{2D_{n}\beta_{n}}{\lambda_{n}}+\frac{16D_{n}^{3}}{\lambda_{n}^{2}}\big]\rightarrow 0.\end{split}

This implies directly that:
\left\|nT_{n}(X^{M})-\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(X^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(X^{M})\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0
.
Following the exact same road map we can show that:
\left\|nT_{n}(Z^{M})-\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(Z^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(Z^{M}_{1:n})\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0
.

Therefore we have

\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(nT_{n}(Z^{M}),\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(Z^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(Z^{M})\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0;

\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(nT_{n}(Y^{M}),\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(Y^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(Y^{M})\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.

Hence using
lemma

10
we know that to establish the desired result it is sufficient to study the asymptotics of
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j\leq n}H_{i,j}^{*\theta_{k}}(Z^{M})\omega^{*}_{k}(Z^{M})
. Hence we can suppose without loss of generality that all the Kernels
(K_{\theta_{i}})
have ranks of
l_{n}
or less, and do so

As the distribution of
X_{i,1}^{M}
is the same than the one of
X_{i,2}^{M}
they also have the same mean embedding. Moreover we note that for all
c\in\mathbb{R}
we have :

\begin{split}H_{i,j}^{\theta_{k}}=&\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\big[\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,1})-\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,2})\big]\big[\psi_{l,k}(X_{j,1}^{n})-\psi_{l,k}(X_{j,2}^{n})\big]\\
&=\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\big[\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,1})+c-\big(\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,2})+c\big)\big]\big[\big(\psi_{l,k}(X_{j,1}^{n})+c\big)-\big(\psi_{l,k}(X_{j,2}^{n})+c\big)\big].\end{split}

Therefore as the test statistics
\hat{T_{n}}
depends only on
(H_{i,j}^{\theta_{k}})
we can suppose without loss of generality that

\mathbb{E}(\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{1,1}))=\mathbb{E}(\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{1,2}))=0,\quad\forall\leq l_{n},~k\leq p_{n}.

We define
X_{i}^{*,n}:=(X_{i,l,k}^{*})_{l,k}
to be random variables, defined as

\begin{split}X_{i,l,k}^{*}:=&\psi_{l,k}(X^{n}_{i,1})-\psi_{l,k}(X^{n}_{i,2}).\end{split}

We define the process
X^{*,n}:=\big(X_{i}^{*,n}\big)
and note that the observations
(X^{*,n}_{i})
take value in
M_{p_{n}\times l_{n}}(\mathbb{R})
. We remark that we could have taken the observations
(X^{*,n}_{i})
to take value in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}l_{n}}
as there is a one to one mapping from
M_{p_{n}\times l_{n}}(\mathbb{R})
to
\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}l_{n}}
. However for ease of notations we keep them as defined.
Let
(Z^{*}_{i})
and
(Y^{*}_{i})
be defined in the following way:

Z^{*}_{i}:=\big(\psi_{l,k}(Z^{M}_{i,1})-\psi_{l,k}(Z^{M}_{i,2})\big)\quad{\rm and}\quad Y^{*}_{i}:=\big(\psi_{l,k}(Y^{M}_{i,1})-\psi_{l,k}(Y^{M}_{i,2})\big).

We note that they form
respectively a bootstrap sample and an independent copy of
(X^{*}_{i})
. Moreover we remark that
Z_{i,1}^{M}|X^{n}\overset{d}{=}Z_{i,2}^{M}|X^{n}
therefore we can also assume with out loss of generality that
\mathbb{E}(Z^{*}_{i,l,k}|X^{n})=0
. We choose
(g_{n})
to be the following sequence of functions:

g_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\sum_{k}\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k}\Big)^{2}

where we have set
\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})=\frac{e^{\beta_{n}h_{n,k}(x_{1:n})}}{\sum_{k}e^{\beta_{n}h_{n,k}(x_{1:n})}}
and

h_{n,k}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}\Big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k}\Big)^{2}}{\frac{4}{n^{3}}\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}(\sum_{i}x_{i,l,k})^{3}-\frac{4}{n^{4}}(\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}(\sum_{i}x_{i,l,k})^{2})^{2}+\lambda_{n}}.

We remark that we have

g_{n}(Z^{*}_{1:n})=nT_{n}(Z^{M}),\quad g_{n}(\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1:n})=nT_{n}(Y^{M}),\quad g_{n}(X^{*}_{1:n})=nT_{n}(X^{M}).

It is therefore enough to study
g_{n}
and
(X^{*}_{{1:n}})
.
For ease of notations we write:

\widehat{\sigma_{n,k}}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{4}{n^{3}}\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}(\sum_{i}x_{i,l,k})^{3}-\frac{4}{n^{4}}(\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}(\sum_{i}x_{i,l,j})^{2})^{2}+\lambda_{n};

(284)

and

\overline{x_{l,k_{1}}}:=\frac{1}{{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k_{1}},\quad\hat{H}_{n,k}(x_{1:n}):=\sum_{l}\lambda_{l,k}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k}\Big)^{2}.

(285)

Moreover we note that we have

\begin{split}\big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}[X^{*}_{1,l,k}]^{2}\|_{L_{120}}&\overset{(a)}{\leq}2\big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\Big([\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,1})]^{2}+[\psi_{l,k}(X^{M}_{i,2})]^{2}\Big)\|_{L_{120}}\\
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}4\big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}K_{\theta_{k}}(X_{1,1}^{M},X_{1,1}^{M})\big\|_{L_{120}}\end{split}

where (a) and (b) come from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Similarly we have:

\begin{split}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\left|X^{*}_{1,l,k}\right|\right\|_{L_{120}}&\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}}\sqrt{\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\left|X^{*}_{1,l,k}\right|^{2}\right\|_{L_{120}}}\\
&\leq 2\sqrt{\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}}\sqrt{\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}K_{\theta_{k}}(X_{1,1}^{M},X_{1,1}^{M})\right\|_{L_{120}}}\end{split}

where (a) comes from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Therefore using
Lemma

8
we note that there is a constant
C
such that:

\displaystyle\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sup_{z\in[\bar{X}^{n},Z_{1}^{*}]\cup[\bar{X}^{n},\tilde{Y}_{1}^{*}]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\hat{H}_{n,k}(Z^{*,i,z})\Big\|_{L_{120}}

(286)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{2}{n^{3/2}}\big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}[X^{*}_{1,l,k}]^{2}\|_{L_{120}}+2\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l\leq n}Z_{l}^{*,n,i})^{2}\Big\|_{L_{120}}

(287)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}C\log(p_{n})\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}K_{\theta_{k}}(X_{1,1}^{M},X_{1,1}^{M})\big\|_{L_{120}}.

(288)

where (a) is a consequence of the triangle inequality and (b) of
Lemma

8
and
section

I.1
. Similarly using
Lemma

8
and
section

I.1
we can establish that there is a constant
C^{\prime}
such that:

\displaystyle\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sup_{z\in[\bar{X}^{n},Z_{1}^{*}]\cup[\bar{X}^{n},\tilde{Y}_{1}^{*}]}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\Big|z_{l,k}+\sum_{i\geq 2}Z_{i,l,k}^{*}\Big|\Big\|_{L_{120}}

(289)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\log(p_{n})\sqrt{\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\lambda_{l,k}}\sqrt{\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}K_{\theta_{k}}(X_{1,1}^{M},X_{1,1}^{M})\right\|_{L_{120}}}

(290)

To prove the desired result we use
Theorem

13
. We remark that the functions
(h_{n,k})
and
(g_{n})
are three times differentiable. This implies that
(H_{0})
hold. We check that
(H_{1}^{*})
also holds.
In this goal we first check that the partial derivatives of
(h_{n,k})
are bounded. For ease of notations for a function
f_{n}
we shorthand:

\partial_{i,k,l}f_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\partial_{x_{i,k,l}}f_{n}(x_{1:n}),\quad\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\partial_{x_{i,k_{1},l_{1}},x_{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})

\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\partial_{x_{i,k_{1},l_{1}},x_{i,k_{2},l_{2}}x_{i,k_{3},l_{3}}}^{3}f_{n}(x_{1:n}).

In this goal, using the chain rule we note that
for all
k\leq p_{n}
and all
l\leq l_{n}
we have:

\displaystyle\partial_{{i,k,l}}\hat{\sigma}_{n,k}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{\lambda_{l,k}}{n}\left(12\overline{x_{l,k}}^{2}-16\overline{x_{l,k}}\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k}(x_{1:n})}{n}\right)

(291)

and:

\begin{split}\partial_{{i,k,l}}h_{n,k}(x_{1:n})=&\frac{2}{{n}}\frac{\lambda_{l,k}\overline{x_{l,k}}}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k}}(x_{1:n})}-\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k,l}}\widehat{\sigma}_{n,k}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k}}(x_{1:n})^{2}}.\end{split}

Therefore we obtain that there is a constant
K
such that:

\begin{split}{n}\left\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\left|\partial_{{i,k,l}}h_{n,k}(Z^{n,i,\bar{X}^{n}})\right|\right\|_{L_{12}}\leq K\Big[\frac{D_{n}}{\lambda_{n}}+\frac{D_{n}^{2}+D_{n}^{3}}{\lambda_{n}^{2}}\Big]\end{split}

Using once again the chain rule we have that:

\begin{split}\partial_{{i,k,l}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})=&\beta_{n}\partial_{{i,k,l}}h_{n,k}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\left(\mathbb{I}(k^{\prime}=k)-\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})\right)\end{split}

as well as:

\begin{split}\partial_{{i,k,l}}g_{n}(x_{1:n})=&\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{l,k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k})\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})+\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\hat{H}_{n,k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k,l}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n}).\end{split}

Therefore, using
section

I.1
and
section

I.1
and as
\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})=1

we obtain that there is a constant
K_{2}
that do not depend on
n
such that

\begin{split}&\sup_{i\leq n}\Big\|\big\|\partial_{{i,k,l}}g_{n}(Z^{*,i,\bar{X}^{n}}_{1:n})\big\|_{v,p_{n}\times l_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\\
&\leq\beta_{n}\sup_{i\leq n}\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\hat{H}_{n,k}(Z^{*,i,\bar{X^{n}}})\big|\Big\|_{L_{24}}\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\sqrt{n}\big|\partial_{{i,k,l}}h_{n,k}(Z^{*,i,\bar{X^{n}}})\big|\Big\|_{L_{24}}\\
&\quad+\sup_{i\leq n}\Big\|\sup_{k\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l\leq l_{n}}\big|\lambda_{l,k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}Z^{*,i,\bar{X^{n}}}_{j,l,k})\big|\Big\|_{L_{12}}\\
&\leq\frac{K_{2}\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})D_{n}^{4}}{\sqrt{n}\min(\lambda_{n}^{2},1)}.\end{split}

This implies that
R^{*}_{n,1}:=O(\frac{\beta_{n}\log(p_{n})D_{n}^{4}}{n^{1/6}\min(\lambda_{n}^{2},1)}).

Moreover all
k_{1},l_{1},k_{2},l_{2}
using the chain rule we have:
\partial_{{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})=0
if
k_{1}
is distinct from
k_{2}
. Moreover if
k_{1}=k_{2}
we have:

\displaystyle\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}\hat{\sigma}_{n,k}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}}{n^{2}}\left(24\overline{x_{l_{1},k_{1}}}\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2})-16\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}{n}\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2})-16\lambda_{k_{2},l_{2}}\overline{x_{l_{1},k_{1}}}\overline{x_{l_{2},k_{1}}}\right)

(292)

and by the Chain rule we have:

\begin{split}\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})&=\frac{2\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\Big[\frac{\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2})}{n}+\frac{\overline{x_{l_{2},k_{1}}}\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\Big]\\
&-\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}h_{n,k_{2}}(x_{1:n})}{n}\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}-\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}{n}\frac{\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\\
&+2\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}{n}\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})^{3}}.\end{split}

Therefore there is a constant
K_{3}
such that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{k_{1:2}\leq p_{n}}\sum_{l_{1:2}\leq l_{n}}\left|\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(Z^{*,i,\bar{X}^{n}}_{1:n})\right|\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{K_{3}D_{n}^{5}}{\max(\lambda_{n}^{3},1)n^{2}}.\end{split}

By another application of the chain rule we have:

\begin{split}\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})=&\beta_{n}\left(\mathbb{I}(k^{\prime}=k_{1})-\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\right)\\
&\times\left(\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})+\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\right)\\
-&\beta_{n}\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\end{split}

by the chain rule we also have that:

\begin{split}\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}g_{n}(x_{1:n})=&\frac{2}{{n}}\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k_{1}})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\\
&+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{k_{2},l_{2}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,k_{2},l_{2}})\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\\
&+\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\hat{H}_{n,k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\end{split}

Therefore we know that there are constants
K_{3}
such that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|\big\|\partial^{2}_{x_{1}}g_{n}(Z^{*,i,\bar{X^{n}}}_{1:n})\big\|_{m,p_{n}\times l_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{K_{3}D_{n}^{7}}{\min(\lambda_{n}^{4},1)n}\beta_{n}^{2}.\end{split}

This implies that
R_{n,2}^{*}=O(\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}D_{n}^{7}}{\min(\lambda_{n}^{4},1)\sqrt{n}})
. Finally using a similar line of reasoning we note that Moreover all
k_{1},l_{1},k_{2},l_{2},l_{3},k_{3}
using the chain rule we have:
\partial_{{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}}^{3}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})=0
if
k_{1}
is distinct from
k_{2}
or from
k_{3}
. Moreover if
k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3}
we have:

\displaystyle\partial_{i,l_{1:3},k_{1:3}}^{3}\hat{\sigma}_{n,k}(x_{1:n})=\frac{\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}}{n^{3}}\Big(

\displaystyle 24\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2}=l_{3})-32\lambda_{k_{1},l_{3}}\overline{x_{l_{3},k_{1}}}\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2})

(293)

\displaystyle-16\lambda_{k_{2},l_{2}}(\overline{x_{l_{1},k_{1}}}\mathbb{I}(l_{3}=l_{2})+\overline{x_{l_{2},k_{1}}}\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{3}))\Big)

(294)

and by the Chain rule we have:

\begin{split}&\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\\
=&-\frac{\partial_{l_{3},k_{3}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})^{2}}\Big(\frac{\overline{x_{l_{1},k_{1}}}\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\big[2\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}+3\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\Big]\\
-&\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\hat{H}_{n,k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}-h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})+2\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}\frac{\mathbb{I}(l_{1}=l_{2})}{n}\Big)\\
+&\frac{2\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})^{2}}\left(\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})\mathbb{I}(l_{2}=l_{3})}{n}+\overline{x_{l_{3},k_{1}}}\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})\right)\\
-&\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}h_{n,k_{2}}(x_{1:n})}{n}\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}-\frac{\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{1},l_{1},k_{3},l_{3}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\Big(\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}h_{n,k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\\
&-{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\Big)-\frac{2\lambda_{l_{3},k_{1}}\bar{X}_{l_{3},k_{1}}}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\left(\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})-\frac{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\right)\\
&-\frac{\hat{H}_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})}{n\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\left(\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})-\frac{\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}^{2}\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}{\widehat{\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(x_{1:n})}\right)\end{split}

Therefore there is a constant
K_{3}
such that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{k_{1:3}\leq p_{n}}\sup_{z\in[\bar{X}^{n},Z_{i}^{*}]\cup[\bar{X}^{n},\tilde{Y}^{*}_{i}]}\sum_{l_{1:3}\leq l_{n}}\left|\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}h_{n,k_{1}}(Z^{*,i,z}_{1:n})\right|\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{K_{3}D_{n}^{7}}{\max(\lambda_{n}^{4},1)n^{3}}.\end{split}

By another application of the chain rule we have:

\begin{split}\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})=&\beta_{n}\left(\mathbb{I}(k^{\prime}=k_{1})-\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\right)\\
&\times\Big({\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})+\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{i,k_{3},l_{3}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})}\\
&\quad+{\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})+\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}},i,k_{3},l_{3}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})}\Big)\\
-&\beta_{n}\partial_{i,k_{3},l_{3}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\\
&\times\left(\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})-\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\right)\\
-&\beta_{n}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\\
&\times\Big(\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}},i,k_{3},l_{3}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})+\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}h_{n,k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\partial^{2}_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\Big)\end{split}

as well as:

\begin{split}&\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}g_{n}(x_{1:n})\\
=&\frac{2}{{n}}\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3})\partial_{i,k_{3},l_{3}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,l,k_{1}})\partial_{{i,k_{2:3},l_{2:3}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\\
&+\frac{2\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{3})}{n}\lambda_{k_{1},l_{1}}\partial_{{i,k_{2},l_{2}}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})+\frac{2\mathbb{I}(k_{2}=k_{3})}{{n}}\lambda_{k_{2},l_{2}}\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}}}\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\\
&+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{k_{2},l_{2}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,k_{2},l_{2}})\partial_{{i,k_{1},l_{1}},i,k_{3},l_{3}}^{2}\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\\
&+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\lambda_{k_{3},l_{3}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\leq n}x_{j,k_{3},l})\partial_{i,k_{1:2},l_{1:2}}^{2}\omega_{k_{3}}(x_{1:n})+\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\hat{H}_{n,k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\partial_{i,k_{1:3},l_{1:3}}^{3}\omega_{k^{\prime}}(x_{1:n})\end{split}

Therefore we know that there are constants
K_{4}
such that:

\begin{split}&\max_{i}\Big\|\max_{z\in[\bar{X^{n}},Z^{*}_{i}]\cup[\bar{X^{n}},\tilde{Y}^{*}_{i}]}\big\|\partial^{2}_{x_{1}}g_{n}(Z^{*,i,z}_{1:n})\big\|_{t,p_{n}\times l_{n}}\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{K_{4}D_{n}^{10}}{\min(\lambda_{n}^{6},1)n^{2}}\beta_{n}^{3}\end{split}

Therefore we have
R_{n,3}^{*}=O(\frac{D_{n}^{10}\beta_{n}^{3}}{\min(\lambda_{n}^{6},1)n})
.

This implies that both
(H_{0})
and
(H_{1}^{*})
hold and
Theorem

13
guarantees that

\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big(n\hat{T}_{n}(Z^{M}),g_{n}(Y^{M})\mid X^{n}\Big)\Big\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.

∎

Appendix J
Proof of
7

We divide the proof of the two statements of
7
in two. Firstly we prove that the bootstrap method is consistent if
\beta_{n}=o(\sqrt{n-m_{n}})
.

Proof.

Let
(Y^{n}_{i})
be an independent copy of
(X^{n}_{i})
.We shorthand

\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\frac{e^{-\beta_{n}{\cal R}^{k}_{n}(x_{1:n})}}{\sum_{k^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}e^{-\beta_{n}{\cal R}^{k^{\prime}}_{n}(x_{1:n})}},\qquad\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}:=\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(X_{1:m_{n}}^{n});

\hat{\Theta}_{n}:=\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X_{1:n}^{n}),\qquad\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}:=\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X_{1:m_{n}}^{n}Y^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n}).

The first step of the proof is to realize that as conditionally on
\hat{\Theta}_{n}
and
\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}
the observations
\left(\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)-\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)\right)_{i\geq 0}
are independent and identically distributed we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\sqrt{{\rm var}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X_{i+m_{n}}^{n},~\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)-\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)\Big|\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y},\hat{\Theta}_{n}\Big]}\right\|_{L_{1}}\\
&=\left\|\sqrt{{\rm var}\Big[\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X_{m_{n}+1}^{n},~\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right)-\mathcal{L}_{n}\Big(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1},\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\Big)\Big|\hat{\Theta}_{n},\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}\Big]}\right\|_{L_{1}}\end{split}

Moreover by exploiting Taylor expansions we know that

\begin{split}&\left\|\sqrt{{\rm var}\Big[\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X_{m_{n}+1}^{n},~\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right)-\mathcal{L}_{n}\Big(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1},\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\Big)\Big|\hat{\Theta}_{n},\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}\Big]}\right\|_{L_{1}}\\
&\leq\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\Big\|\sup_{\theta\in\Omega(\{\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n},~p\leq p_{n}\})}\left|\partial_{2,l}\mathcal{L}_{n}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1},\theta(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1}))\right|\Big\|_{L_{\infty}}\times\Big\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{Y}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\Big\|_{L_{2}}\\
&\leq L_{n}d^{\prime}_{n}\sup_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\Big\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{Y}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\Big\|_{L_{2}}\end{split}

We define
(X_{i}^{n,j})
as the following interpolating process:
X_{i}^{n,j}:=\begin{cases}X_{i}^{n}~{\rm if}~i\leq j\\
Y_{j}^{n}~{\rm otherwise}\end{cases}
and shorthand
\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{j}:=\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}(X_{1:n}^{n,j})
. By the Effron-Stein inequality we have:

\begin{split}\left\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{Y}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}&\overset{}{\leq}\sum_{j\geq m_{n}+1}\left\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{j}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{j-1}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\\
&\leq(n-m_{n})\left\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{n}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{n-1}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right\|_{L_{2}}^{2}\end{split}

Moreover using the triangle inequality and the inequality
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
we can upper-bound the right-hand side as

\begin{split}&\left\|\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{n}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})-\hat{\Theta}_{n,l}^{n-1}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1})\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\\
&=\left\|\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n,l}\left(\omega_{n}^{p}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n})-\omega_{n}^{p}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n-1}Y^{n}_{n})\right)\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\\
\leq&2\left\|\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n,l}\omega_{n}^{p}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n})\Big[e^{\frac{\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}[\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{n}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p})-\mathcal{L}_{n}(Y_{n}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p})]}-1\Big]\right\|_{L_{2}}^{2}\\
&~+2\left\|\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n,l}\omega_{n}^{p}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n})\sum_{p^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\omega_{n}^{p^{\prime}}(X^{n}_{m_{n}+1:n})\Big[e^{\frac{\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}[\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{n}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p^{\prime}})-\mathcal{L}_{n}(Y_{n}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p^{\prime}})]}-1\Big]\right\|_{L_{2}}^{2}\\
&\leq 4\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}}{(n-m_{n})}\|\sup_{p\leq p_{n}}|\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p}|\|^{2}_{L_{\infty}}\left\|\sup_{p\leq p_{n}}\left|\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{1}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p})\right|\right\|^{2}_{L_{\infty}}e^{\frac{2\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\left\|\sup_{p\leq p_{n}}\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{1}^{n},\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p})\right\|_{L_{\infty}}}\\
&\leq 4\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}}{(n-m_{n})}T^{2}_{n}L^{2}_{n}e^{\frac{2\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}L_{n}}\end{split}

By combining
appendix

J

and
appendix

J
we therefore obtain that:

\begin{split}&\left\|\sqrt{{\rm var}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X_{i+m_{n}}^{n},~\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)-\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)\Big|\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Y},\hat{\Theta}_{n}\Big]}\right\|_{L_{1}}\\
&\longrightarrow 0.\end{split}

Hence by using the fact that
d_{{\cal F}}
satisfies the triangle inequality (see
Lemma

10
) we observe that it is enough to prove that the distribution of

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)
can be correctly approximated by the bootstrap method. We note that the process
\left(\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)\right)
is exchangeable and that if we define the function:
g_{n}(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{i}
then:

g_{n}\left(\left(\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right)\right)_{1:n}\right):=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}},\hat{\Theta}^{Y}_{n}(X^{n}_{i+m_{n}})\right).

We remark that
(g_{n})
satisfy all the conditions of
Theorem

14
which implies that the desired result holds.

∎

Appendix K
Proof of
8

For simplicity we write

\begin{split}&T_{l,n}:=\left\|\sup_{p\leq p_{n}}\left|\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n,l}(X_{1:m_{n}}^{n})(Y_{1}^{n,1})\right|\right\|_{L_{\infty}}\lor 1,\\
&L^{*}_{l_{1:i},n}:=\big\|\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\leq p_{n}\end{subarray}}\big|\partial^{i}_{2,l_{1:i}}\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{n}^{n},\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(X^{n}_{1:m_{n}})(X_{n}^{n}))\big|\big\|_{L_{\infty}}\lor 1.\end{split}

Proof.

For ease of notations, we shorthand:

\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}:=\hat{\Theta}_{n}(X^{n}_{1:m_{n}}Z^{n,2}_{1:n-m_{n}}),\qquad\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}:=\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(X_{1:m_{n}}^{n}).

We first notice using
lemma

10
and
lemma

11
that:

\begin{split}&\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Z_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\left({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Z_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}},X^{n}\right),\\
&\qquad\quad{\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\left({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)\mid X^{n}\Big)\Big\|_{L_{1}}\\
&\leq\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Z_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\big({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Z_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}},X^{n}\big)\\
&~\qquad\qquad,{\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\big({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}\big)\Big)\Big\|_{L_{1}}\\
&+\Big\|d_{{\cal F}}\Big({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\left({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}\right),\\
&\qquad\qquad{\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})-\mathbb{E}\left({\cal R}^{{\rm s}}_{\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1})\mid\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)\mid X^{n}\Big)\Big\|_{L_{1}}\end{split}

We upper-bound each term seperately. In this goal, we define as
(X^{\prime}_{i})
the process defined as
X^{\prime}_{i}=\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{i+m_{n}}^{n},\hat{\Theta}^{Z^{n,2}}_{n}).
We remark that the sequence
(X^{\prime}_{i})
is an exchangeable sequence and we define:

Z^{\prime}_{i}:=\mathcal{L}_{n}(Z^{n,1}_{i},\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z,n2}),\quad Y^{\prime}_{i}:=\mathcal{L}_{n}(Y^{n,1}_{i},\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z,n2}).

We note that
(Z^{\prime}_{i})
and
(Y^{\prime}_{i})
respectively form a bootstrap sample of
(X^{\prime}_{i})_{i\leq n-m_{n}}
and a copy of
(X^{\prime}_{i})
that is conditionally on
\hat{\Theta}_{n}^{Z^{n,2}}
independent. We define
F_{1,n}
to be the following functions:

F_{1,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}x_{i}.

Moreover we denote
(X^{*}_{i})
the following random vectors
X^{*}_{i}=\Big(\mathcal{L}_{n}(X_{i+m_{n}}^{n},\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}\Big)_{p\leq p_{n}}.
We also define:

Z^{*}_{i}=\Big(\mathcal{L}_{n}(Z_{i+m_{n}}^{n,2},\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}\Big)_{p\leq p_{n}},\quad Z^{*}_{i}=\Big(\mathcal{L}_{n}(Y_{i+m_{n}}^{n,2},\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}\Big)_{p\leq p_{n}}.

We remark that
(Z^{*}_{i})
and
(Y^{*}_{i})
are respectively bootstrap samples and (conditionally) independent copy of
(X^{*}_{i})
.

We shorthand

R_{n}^{p}:=\mathbb{E}(\mathcal{L}_{n}(X^{n}_{p},\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n})|\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n});\qquad\bar{X^{\prime}_{p}}:=\frac{1}{n-m_{n}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}X^{\prime}_{i,p}.

We define the following weight functions:
\omega_{n}^{p}:\times_{i=1}^{n-m_{n}}\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
as:

\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\frac{e^{\frac{-\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}x_{i,p}}}{\sum_{p^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}e^{\frac{-\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}x_{i,p^{\prime}}}}.

We define

F_{2,n}:\times_{l=1}^{n}\mathbb{R}^{p_{n}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
as the following random function:

\begin{split}F_{2,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}\sum_{i\leq n-m_{n}}&\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(Y^{n,1}_{i},\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(Y_{i}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\right)\\
&-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(Y^{n,1}_{i},\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(Y_{i}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\right)\mid X^{n}\right).\end{split}

We remark that, conditionally on
X^{n}
and
Y^{n,1}
, the functions
(F_{2,n})
are independent from
(Z^{*}_{i})
and
(Y^{*}_{i})
.
Using
appendix

K
we note that if the following hold then the desired result also holds:

\begin{split}\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(F_{2,n}(Z^{*}_{1:n-m_{n}}),F_{2,n}(\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1:n-m_{n}})|X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0;\end{split}

\begin{split}\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(F_{1,n}(Z^{\prime}_{1:n-m_{n}}),F_{1,n}(\tilde{Y}^{\prime}_{1:n-m_{n}})\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.\end{split}

To prove that those hold we use
Theorem

13
extended to exchangeable sequences and random functions. We notice that the functions
(F_{2,n})
and
(F_{1,n})
are three times differentiable (random) functions. Therefore for both functions hypothesis
(H_{0})
holds and to get the desired results we only need to check that hypothesis
(H_{1}^{*})
also holds. For simplicity for all
i\leq 3
we write

\begin{split}\hat{R}_{j}^{n,l_{1:i}}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}\Big(&\partial_{2,l_{1:i}}^{i}\mathcal{L}_{n}\Big({Y^{n,1}_{j},\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(Y_{j}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}\Big)\\
&-\mathbb{E}\Big({\partial_{2,l_{1:i}}^{i}\mathcal{L}_{n}\big({Y^{n,1}_{j},\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}^{p}_{n}(Y_{j}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}\big)\mid X^{n}}\Big)\Big).\end{split}

We define the following random functions:
f_{l_{1:3},x}(z):=\partial_{2,l_{1:3}}^{3}\mathcal{L}_{n}\left(z,\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}_{n}^{p}(z)\omega_{p}(xZ^{*}_{2:n-m_{n}}))\right);
and remark that the set of functions
\{f_{l_{1:3},x},~l_{1:3}\leq d^{\prime}_{n},x\in[0,Z^{*}_{1}]\bigcup[0,Y^{*}_{1}]\}
has an epsilon covering number proportional to
\big(\sum_{l\leq n}L^{*}_{l,n}T_{l,n}\beta_{n}/((n-m_{n})\epsilon)\big)^{p_{n}}p_{n}
. This implies that there is a constant
K
such that we have:

\begin{split}\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\sup_{x\in[\bar{X}^{n,*},Z^{*}_{1}]\bigcup[\bar{X}^{n,*},\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1}]}\left|\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{j}^{n,l_{1:3}}(Z^{*,i,x}_{1:n})\right|\right\|_{L_{72}}\leq{K\log(p_{n})}L^{*}_{n,l_{1:3}}.\end{split}

Moreover we note that for all
k\leq 2
there is a constant
C
such that

\begin{split}\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\left|\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{j}^{n,l_{1:k}}(Z^{*,i,\overline{Z^{*}}}_{1:n})\right|\right\|_{L_{72}}\leq{C}L^{*}_{n,l_{1:k}}.\end{split}

For ease of notations we write:
\overline{\theta^{p}_{n,l}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}},y):=\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p}(y)-\sum_{p^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p^{\prime}}(y)\omega_{n}^{p^{\prime}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}).

We remark that for all
p\leq p_{n}
we have

\begin{split}&\partial_{i,p}F_{2,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\\
=&-\frac{\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\omega^{p}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\Big[\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p}(Y_{j}^{n,1})-\sum_{p^{\prime}\leq p_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p^{\prime}}(Y_{j}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p^{\prime}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\Big]\\
=&-\frac{\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\omega^{p}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p}_{n,l}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}},Y_{j}^{n,1})\end{split}

Therefore as
\sum_{p\leq p_{n}}\omega_{n}^{p}(x_{1:n})=1
, using
appendix

K
we obtain that there is a constant
C
that does not depend on
n
such that

\begin{split}&\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\left\|\partial_{i}F_{2,n}(Z^{*,i,\overline{X^{*}}}_{1:n-m_{n}})\right\|_{v,p_{n}}\right\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{C\beta_{n}}{n-m_{n}}\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}L^{*}_{l,n}\Big\|\max_{p\leq p_{n}}\big|\hat{\theta}_{n,l}^{p}(Y_{1}^{n,1})\big|\Big\|_{L_{12}}.\end{split}

Moreover for all
p\leq p_{n}
we have
\partial_{i}F_{1,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}.

Therefore we obtain that

\begin{split}&\max_{i}\Big\|\partial_{i}F_{1,n}(Z^{{}^{\prime},i,\overline{X^{\prime}}}_{1:n-m_{n}})\Big\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n-m_{n}}}.\end{split}

In addition, we remark that we have
\partial_{i}^{2}F_{1,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})=0
. Therefore the condition
(H_{1}^{*})
holds for
F_{1,n}
. For ease of notations we write
\omega^{p_{1},p_{2}}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\mathbb{I}(p_{1}=p_{2})-\omega_{n}^{p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}}).
We have

\begin{split}&\partial_{i,p_{1},p_{2}}^{2}F_{2,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\\
&=\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}{(n-m_{n})^{2}}\omega^{p_{1},p_{2}}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{1}}_{n,l}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\\
&+\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega_{n}^{p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}{(n-m_{n})^{2}}\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l_{1},l_{2}}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{1}}_{n,l_{1}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\overline{\theta^{p_{2}}_{n,l_{2}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\\
&-\frac{\beta_{n}^{2}}{(n-m_{n})^{2}}\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega_{n}^{p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}^{n,j}_{l}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{2}}_{n,l}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1}).\end{split}

This combined with
appendix

K
implies that there is a constant
C_{2}
such that:

\begin{split}&\max_{i}\left\|\left\|\partial_{i}^{2}F_{2,n}(Z^{*,i,\overline{X^{*}}}_{1:n-m_{n}})\right\|_{m,p_{n}}\right\|_{L_{12}}\leq\frac{C_{2}\beta_{n}^{2}}{(n-m_{n})^{2}}\Big[\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}L^{*}_{l,n}T_{l,n}+\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}L^{*}_{l_{1},l_{2},n}T_{l_{1},n}T_{l_{2},n}\Big]\end{split}

Finally using the chain rule for all
p_{1},p_{2},p_{3}\leq p_{n}
we have

\begin{split}&\partial_{i,p_{1:3}}^{3}F_{2,n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\\
&=\frac{\beta_{n}^{3}\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{1}}_{n,l_{1}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}{(n-m_{n})^{3}}\Big[\omega_{n}^{p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega^{p_{2},p_{3}}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\\
&-\omega_{n}^{p^{\prime}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega^{p_{1},p_{3}}_{n}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\Big]-\frac{\beta_{n}^{3}\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}{(n-m_{n})^{3}}\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l_{1},l_{2}}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{1}}_{n,l_{1}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\\
&\quad\times\overline{\theta^{p_{3}}_{n,l_{3}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\Big[\omega_{n}^{p_{3}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega_{n}^{p_{1},p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})+\omega_{n}^{p_{3}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega_{n}^{p_{1},p_{2}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\Big]\\
&-\frac{\beta_{n}^{3}\prod_{i=1}^{3}\omega_{n}^{p_{i}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})}{(n-m_{n})^{3}}\Big[\sum_{l_{1:3}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq p_{n}}\hat{R}_{l_{1},l_{2},l_{3}}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\prod_{k=1}^{3}\overline{\theta^{p_{k}}_{n,l_{k}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})-\sum_{l_{1:2}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\hat{R}_{l_{1},l_{2}}^{n,j}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\\
&\quad\times\overline{\theta^{p_{2}}_{n,l_{1}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\overline{\theta^{p_{3}}_{n,l_{2}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\Big]+\frac{\beta_{n}^{3}\omega_{n}^{p_{1}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\omega^{p_{2}}_{n}(x_{1:n})\big[\omega_{n}^{p_{1},p_{3}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})+\omega_{n}^{p_{2},p_{3}}(x_{1:n-m_{n}})\big]}{(n-m_{n})^{3}}\\
&\quad\times\sum_{l\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}\sum_{j\leq m_{n}}\hat{R}_{l}^{n,j}(x_{1:n})\overline{\theta^{p_{2}}_{n,l_{1}}}(x_{1:n},Y_{j}^{n,1})\end{split}

Therefore using
appendix

K
we establish that that there is a constant
C_{3}<\infty
such that

\begin{split}&\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\max_{x\in[\bar{X}^{n},Z^{*}_{1}]\bigcup[\bar{X}^{n},\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1}]}\left\|\partial_{i}^{3}F_{2,n}(Z^{*,i,x}_{1:n-m_{n}})\right\|_{t,p_{n}}\right\|_{L_{12}}\\
&\leq\frac{C_{3}\beta_{n}^{3}\log(p_{n})}{(n-m_{n})^{3}}\sum_{l_{1}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}T_{l_{1},n}\Big[L^{*}_{l_{1},n}+\sum_{l_{2}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}L^{*}_{l_{1:2},n}T_{l_{2},n}+\sum_{l_{2:3}\leq d^{\prime}_{n}}L^{*}_{l_{1:3},n}T_{l_{2},n}T_{l_{3},n}\Big]\end{split}

This implies that hypothesis
(H_{1}^{*})
holds for
F_{2,n}
; which means that

\begin{split}\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(F_{2,n}(Z^{*}_{1:n-m_{n}}),F_{2,n}(\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1:n-m_{n}})|X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0;\end{split}

We note that
F_{2,n}(\tilde{Y}^{*}_{1:n-m_{n}}):=\mathcal{R}^{s}_{\hat{\Theta}^{\prime}_{n}}(Y_{1:n-m_{n}}^{n,1}).

Moreover, we remark that we have proved that the condition
(H_{1}^{*})
also holds for
F_{1,n}
. This implies that we have

\begin{split}\left\|d_{{\cal F}}\left(F_{1,n}(Z^{\prime}_{1:n-m_{n}}),F_{1,n}(\tilde{Y}^{\prime}_{1:n-m_{n}})\mid X^{n}\right)\right\|_{L_{1}}\rightarrow 0.\end{split}

As
F_{1,n}
is linear this gives us the desired result.

∎

Appendix L
Proofs from
Appendix

A

L.1
Proof of
Lemma

8

Proof.

The first step is to bound
\mathbb{E}\Big[\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}\Big]
. Define
(f_{n}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R})
to be the following sequence of functions:

f_{n}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{\log\Big(\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}e^{\log(p_{n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{i,l}}\Big)}{\log(p_{n})}.

We observe that

\|f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{n})-\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}\|_{L_{1}}\leq 1
; and that the functions
(f_{n})
are infinitely differentiable.
We denote, for all
z\in\mathbb{R}
,
(\tilde{X}^{i,n}_{l})
and
(\tilde{X}^{i,z}_{l})
the processes respecting

\tilde{X}^{i,n}_{l}:=\begin{cases}\tilde{X}^{n}_{l}~{\rm if}~i\leq l\\
0~{\rm otherwise}.\end{cases}\qquad\tilde{X}^{i,z}_{l}:=\begin{cases}\tilde{X}^{i,n}_{l}~{\rm if}~i\neq l\\
z~{\rm otherwise}\end{cases}.

We first suppose that
\|\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,k}\|_{L_{p}}\leq 1
.
Using the Taylor expansion we have:

\begin{split}\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{n})\Big]\Big|&\leq\sum_{i\leq n}\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{i,n}_{1:n})-f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{i-1,n}_{1:n})\Big]\Big|\\
&\leq\sum_{i\leq n}\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[~(\tilde{X}^{n}_{i})^{\top}~\partial_{i}f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{i,0}_{1:n})\Big]\Big|+\sum_{i\leq n}\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[(\tilde{X}^{n}_{i})^{\top}\partial^{2}_{i}f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{i,0}_{1:n})\tilde{X}^{n}_{i}\Big]\Big|\\
&+\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sup_{z\in[0,\tilde{X}^{n}_{i}]}\Big<\Big|\partial^{3}_{i}f_{n}(\tilde{X}^{i,z}_{1:n})\Big|,\Big|\tilde{X}^{\otimes 3}_{i}\Big|\Big>\Big]\\
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}n\Big|\sum_{d_{1},d_{2}\leq p_{n}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\partial^{2}_{i,d_{1},d_{2}}F^{n}_{\beta}(\tilde{X}^{i,0}_{1:n})\Big]\mathbb{E}(\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{1}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{2}})\Big|\\
&+n\sum_{d_{1},d_{2},d_{2}\leq n}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sup_{z\in[0,\tilde{X}^{n}_{i}]}\Big|\partial^{3}_{i,d_{1:3}}F^{n}_{\beta}(\tilde{X}^{i,z}_{1:n})\Big|~\Big|\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{1}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{2}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{3}}\Big|\Big]\end{split}

where to get (a) we used the fact that as
\sigma(\tilde{X}^{i,0}_{1:n})=\sigma(\tilde{X}_{0}^{n},\dots,\tilde{X}_{i-1}^{n})
we have
\mathbb{E}(\tilde{X}^{n}_{i}|\tilde{X}^{i,0}_{1:n})=0
. The next step is to upper-hand the right-hand side of
Section

L.1
. For ease of notations we write:

\omega_{k}(x_{1:n}):=\frac{e^{\log(p_{n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{i,k}}}{\sum_{l\leq p_{n}}e^{\log(p_{n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{i,l}}}.

For all
k_{1},k_{2}\leq p_{n}
we obtain by the chain rule that

\begin{split}&\Big|\partial_{i,k_{1},k_{2}}^{2}f_{n}(x_{1:n})\Big|\leq\begin{cases}\frac{\log(p_{n})}{n}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\qquad{\rm if}~k_{1}\neq k_{2}\\
\frac{\log(p_{n})}{n}\Big[\omega_{k_{1}}^{2}(x_{1:n})+\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\Big]\qquad{\rm if}~k_{1}=k_{2}.\end{cases}\end{split}

As
\sum_{k\leq p_{n}}\omega_{k}(x_{1:n})=1
, this implies that

\begin{split}&n\Big|\sum_{d_{1},d_{2}\leq p_{n}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\partial^{2}_{i,d_{1},d_{2}}F_{\beta}(\tilde{X}^{i,0})\Big]\mathbb{E}(\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{1}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{2}})\Big|\leq{2\log(p_{n})}.\end{split}

We now bound the second term of the right hand side of
eq.

97
. For all integers
k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\leq p_{n}
, by the chain rule, we obtain that:

\begin{split}&\left|\partial_{i,k_{1:3}}^{3}F_{\beta}(x^{\prime}_{1:n})\right|\\
&\leq\quad\frac{\log(p_{n})^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{3}}(x_{1:n})\left(1+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{3})+\mathbb{I}(k_{2}=k_{3})\right)\\
&+\frac{\log(p_{n})^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\omega_{k_{2}}(x_{1:n})\left(\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{3})+\mathbb{I}(k_{2}=k_{3})\right)\\
&+\frac{\log(p_{n})^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}\omega_{k_{1}}(x_{1:n})\left(\omega_{k_{3}}(x_{1:n})\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})+\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3})\right)\end{split}

Therefore we obtain that

\begin{split}&n\sum_{d_{1},d_{2},d_{3}\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left(\sup_{z\in[0,\tilde{X}^{n}_{i}]}\left|\partial^{3}_{i,d_{1:3}}F^{\beta,n}(\tilde{X}^{i,z})\right|~\left|\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{1}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{2}}\tilde{X}^{n}_{1,d_{3}}\right|\right)\leq\frac{7\beta^{2}\log(p_{n})^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}.\end{split}

Hence using
section

L.1
we establish that:

\begin{split}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}\right)\right|&\leq 1+\Big[{\log(p_{n})}+\frac{7\log(p_{n})^{2}}{6\sqrt{n}}\Big].\end{split}

By potentially renormalizing
\|\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\left|\tilde{X}_{i,k}^{n}\right|\|_{L_{p}}
we obtain therefore than in general we have:

\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}\right)\right|\\
&\leq\left\|\max_{k\leq p_{n}}|X_{1,k}|\right\|_{L_{p}}\left(1+{\log(p_{n})}+\frac{6\log(p_{n})^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right).\end{split}

Lastly, according to the Rosenthal inequality for martingales
[
30
]
, there are constants
(C_{p})
that do not depend on
d
or
(\tilde{X}^{n}_{i})
such that

\begin{split}&\left\|\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}-\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{k\leq p_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,k}\right)\right\|_{L_{p}}\leq 2C_{p}\left\|\sup_{d_{1}\leq p_{n}}\left|\tilde{X}^{n}_{i,d_{1}}\right|\right\|_{L_{p}}.\end{split}

Therefore we get the desired results using the triangular inequality.

Finally, let
(\mathbb{F}_{i})
designates the filtration
\mathbb{F}_{i}:=\sigma\Big(X^{n}_{1},\dots,X^{n}_{i}\Big)
then the following is an array of martingale differences
\left(\mathbb{E}(g_{k,n}(X^{n})|\mathbb{F}_{i})-\mathbb{E}(g_{k,n}(X^{n})|\mathbb{F}_{i-1})\right)
. The last point of
Lemma

8
follows directly from this observation.
∎
